## L1 fix round (2026-10-14, Codex)

This supersedes the earlier L1 verdict on the FTC-08 radius move. The reviewer
was right: holding the tip while moving the elbow made the radius shaft
non-radial. The earlier statement that the steeper shaft was acceptable was
wrong. Every changed shaft now uses `rim_tip` for its proven site, including
radius measurements; shoulder-only changes preserve the original shaft.
The native FTC-08 regression requires a changed shaft to be exactly radial,
with no part-specific angular margin, and passes in the full release suite.

The search now covers all integer stations within the existing 7 mm row and
tries shoulder fractions and one/two mm extensions. Contact is checked before
expensive projected-ink measurement. Identical shafts are allowed inside the
uncertain silhouette boxes; changed segments still need clearance. Those
conservative boxes (including section/isometric envelopes), individual-edge
crossing vetoes and the strict contiguous-run veto are important reasons why
other contacts remain. The existing no-worse tiers and numeric quality/route thresholds are unchanged;
no part/feature IDs enter the routing rule. The geometry helper uses flat label
coordinates, avoiding allocations on ordinary candidate measurements.

**Measured result against 0419fa6c: 53 → 49 extension-contact pairs**, on three
changed drawings; the other 27 SVGs are byte-identical. The 119-callout rendered
safeguard passes, including `--require-improvement`. Each moved route has zero
hidden/section/cutting-plane crossings and zero contiguous travel beside model,
hidden or extension ink. Shaft plus shoulder, in paper mm:

| Part / callout | Contacts before → after (part) | Route length before → after |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| cgb245 `slot:1/slot_end_radius.radius` | 6 → 4 | 10.000 → 6.780 |
| CTC-01 `slot:1/slot_end_radius.radius` | 5 → 4 | 22.431 → 20.957 |
| FTC-08 `slot:2/slot_end_radius.radius` | 3 → 2 | 10.000 → 7.099 |

FTC-08's revised shaft points exactly towards its arc centre. The retained
location-extension pair is still counted: its filled arrowhead grazes the
extension by 0.028 mm. This below-stroke-width cosmetic contact is retained
under the dense-NIST bar, an overturnable judgement in `next-session.md`, not
claimed as complete clearance. The other two changed callouts clear their
extension contacts in the inspected details.

All 300 immutable CI cells pass. The extended 420-cell audit has no part worse:
ink 52 → 52, feature crossings 73 → 73, hidden-on-visible 2232 → 2232,
text-off-span 5 → 5, unplanned features 42 → 42, claimed faces 1553 → 1553;
off-sheet and repeated spans remain zero. Every dimension's ID, label, value,
claims and state, every table, note and GD&T item, and every problem list are
unchanged. Only callout placement and derived sheet-fill/basis metadata differ;
1004's unused 1:2 candidate reports 9.3% instead of 9.4%, with its chosen sheet
and geometry unchanged. No manufacturing information was withdrawn.

The thirteen unchanged parts with contacts are 10000 (2), 10020 (5), 10033 (1),
10060 (5), 10063 (3), cgb241 (4), CTC-02 (2), CTC-03 (3), CTC-04 (2), FTC-06 (3),
FTC-07 (1), FTC-09 (6), FTC-10 (2). CGB245, CTC-01 and FTC-08 retain 4, 4 and 2
respectively. The absolute zero-run target remains **unmet: 15 original runs**
are unchanged (the preceding L1 table lists them). A moved callout cannot retain
such a run. The unchanged-base safeguard passes; reduction mode fails base at
53, and the safeguard still rejects 0d46bc3 on the named long/hidden/cut/collinear
routes (`target/l1/fix-{base,rejected}-*.log`).

The route safeguard is now called by `ci_acceptance.py` in CI's quality/pin
jobs. It uses the renderer's resolved `line_work` cutting-plane strokes for
any axis, instead of reconstructing a horizontal model-Y cut. It compares
shared callout IDs and checks new IDs for runs, allowing legitimate inventory
changes; Rust coverage tests continue to guard information. Python contracts
exercise length, each crossing class, existing/new runs and a vertical cut.
CI timeout defaults are unchanged; optional wall-budget arguments let this
throttled local machine finish without weakening the engineering bars.

Visual QA: all thirty final SVGs were rendered by the release binary through
`quality.py`, then rasterised with resvg at 2400 px on white. CGB245, CTC-01 and
FTC-08 were inspected as whole sheets and cropped details before/after.

## I2a: render every planned sheet (2026-10-14)

Rendering/API slice of accepted R5, based on `origin/main` d0130dec. The changed
files are `src/model.rs`, `src/api.rs`, `src/output.rs`, `src/title_block.rs`, the
one numbering call in `src/sheets.rs`, new `tests/sheets_render.rs`, and
`docs/model.md`, `docs/next-session.md`, this status file. No commit or push.

`model::render_sheets` returns one Drawing per sheet with a shared projection
cache; `render` keeps its sheet-1 signature. Each sheet draws its own view and
corner/hole-table ink, the shared title block and its planned ISO 7200 `n/m` SHEET
value. All cell boxes remain unchanged. The whole authored Spec stays embedded;
custom sheet labels get the existing replacement info and the resolved label is
checked by the existing font-based overflow warning. Notes, drawing-wide
requirements and projection furniture stay on sheet 1. An auxiliary's planned
scale note is shown when its scale differs from the title block.

API output retains `<stem>.svg` / `.png` on sheet 1, adds `.sheet<n>.svg` / `.png`
for later sheets, and writes one PDF with a page per sheet. The existing atomic
writer already handles these suffixes and dotted output prefixes. `pdf_pages`
uses the existing path/text/font writer per page; one-page bytes stay identical.
Review returns sheet 1 as `svg` plus optional `sheets` for later SVGs. Its lint
and structural verdicts still cover sheet 1; whole-document coverage, per-sheet
structural checks, drawn zones/cross-references remain I2b. MCP sheet operations
remain I3. DXF export remains disabled.

Four acceptance tests were added, and **each fails at runtime on the frozen base**
using the exact final test source and an isolated Cargo target (0/4 pass):

- `every_sheet_has_its_title_block`: base emits one SVG instead of nine; final
  checks every numbered cell/value on A4–A0, unchanged grid boxes, replacement
  info and the existing overwide-value warning.
- `formats_and_written_sheet_files_round_trip_the_whole_spec`: base returns only
  svg/pdf/png; final checks five correctly named outputs for two sheets, two PDF
  pages with distinct content streams, distinct PNG rasters, written bytes and
  the whole Spec round-trip through every file.
- `review_exposes_later_sheets_and_keeps_sheet_one_furniture`: base has no later
  SVGs; final retains sheet-1 notes and puts the assigned table on sheet 2, with
  no extra review field for a single-sheet document.
- `remote_section_has_its_hatch_and_caption_on_sheet_two`: base has one SVG;
  final checks sheet-1 cutting letters, sheet-2 section caption and hatch strokes
  in the section's upper half, away from the caption/title block.

No target was lowered or fixture rewritten. The first shared-target archive check
reused the working library and was discarded; `target/i2a/base-proof.json` proves
that the isolated run uses the original production source and exact final test
file, with runtime failures in `final-base-tests.log`.

Visual example: native `nist/nist_ftc_06_asme1_rd.stp`, `sheets.max: 2`, section A–A
assigned to sheet 2. SVG count **1 -> 2**, PNG count **1 -> 2**, PDF pages **1 -> 2**,
rendered section captions **0 -> 1**; SHEET becomes **1/2** and **2/2**. Both SVGs
were rasterised by resvg at 2400 px and inspected, and both PDF pages opened and
rasterised by Poppler. PDF text extraction finds the numbering and section caption.
The section is hatched at its parent's 1:2 scale, clear of border and title block.
The first sheet retains all 35 planned dimensions, schedules, notes, cutting-plane
furniture and model/hidden ink. The entire Plan matches base except the two sheets'
numbered title values. Its before/after raster differs only in the SHEET cell:
125 pixels, box [2328,1630,2346,1643] on a 2400 x 1697 raster, proving no changed
leaders/crossings/collinearity. The A4 plate's two-sheet preview confirms repeated
title fields, sheet-1 notes/projection and the sheet-2 corner table.

All 30 quality parts were rendered and rasterised: **300/300 cells equal**
`tools/ci-quality-baseline.json`. Totals: ink 52 -> 52, off-sheet 0 -> 0,
repeated labels 7 -> 7, feature crossings 73 -> 73, hidden-on-visible 2232 -> 2232;
other five measured totals remain zero. No information was dropped; the remote
section that base left unrendered now reaches its sheet.

Final checks pass: fmt; strict all-target release Clippy; **63 Rust tests** in the
five selected targets (9 api_analysis, 7 output, 28 sheets including the frozen
**1,876-record** byte guard, 4 sheets_render, 15 title_block); final strengthened
sheets_render recheck **4/4**; Python quality **34/34**, acceptance **21/21**, no
skips; schema generator no diff; MCP **7/7**, 16 calls; quality **300/300** unchanged.
The full Cargo suite and all-job acceptance were not run locally, as instructed.
Original `known_single_sheet.json` unchanged (SHA-256
`88b83ded35028e9e724dda8c04ac1e415e9ba15a9f4a070c8001c5dd8ecb4db7`).
Final release binary SHA-256
`5d97c84d317f266a517ee5e65f38ddbcb8effc2d54836c5277f5281a3152fecf` matches the

## I3: MCP and sessions for multiple sheets (2026-10-14)

Built on frozen main `d0130dec51ad1c748f5484b660c343bea0417648`. Session deltas
patch the `sheets` collection by derived `sheet:n` keys. Item responses and
annotation provenance preserve sheet ownership, including sheet 1 in a multi-sheet
document, without changing default single-sheet bytes. Qualified addresses, local
near searches, `filter.sheet`, view move options, page-selected cached rendering,
SVG fragments, summary sheet details and cumulative `Stats.sheet_steps` are implemented.
The existing sheet-1 Rust methods remain wrappers. Move operations remain ordinary
Spec `set` edits; planner/correction/op budgets are unchanged.

Schemas admit R5 controls and address/page inputs. Equivalent schema compaction
keeps all validation constraints and `$defs/op` on apply/preview inputs: total
served schemas are 39,211 -> 39,495 bytes, below the unchanged 39,500-byte gate.
The independent schema checker now exercises the multi-sheet contract as well.

on the frozen base before implementation (`target/i3-base-sheets-mcp.log`). They
were subsequently strengthened with assertions for the new Rust page methods,
replay/provenance, overlays, relation consequences and strict wire decoding.

(`target/i3-base-latency.log`: absent `sheet_steps`). The extended independent
schema checker fails on base's closed Spec shape (`target/i3-base-independent-schemas.log`).

with the base/changed Plans equal and CLI SVG bytes equal. Base sessions co-plotted
sheet-2 geometry on sheet 1; page selection now removes those overlaps and draws
the section with its hatch on sheet 2. All 11 / 14 dimensions remain on sheet 1.
Both changed pages were rendered to SVG and rasterised at 2400 px with resvg,
then inspected (`target/i3-visual/`). Every quality-manifest part was rendered with
the release CLI and rasterised too. Later-sheet n/m, zones and cross-reference
text remain I2's furniture work; the session selector supplies its allocated page
and cached ink. No DXF was produced.

8 scoped targets (including all 28 sheets tests and the 1,876-record byte/refusal
guard); Python quality 34 and CI acceptance 21, no skips; independent validation
of 12 tools / 24 schemas including the new sheet cases; regenerated schemas leave

apart from the intentional compiled-engine part_ref namespace. Existing maximum

## Stream G3: draw one chosen part of an assembly (2026-10-15)

Merged g/g3-draw-assembly-part (61a47a83) onto main d95f5709 without conflicts. quiddity and
haecceity are pinned to quiddity-rust rev 0d601d20 (haecceity slice 1, part definitions) until
the next tag. New: `analyse_*_part` and `parts()` in model.rs, a `part` argument through the
API, session, CLI (`draftwright parts STEP`, `--part K`) and MCP (`parts` tool, `part` on
inspect_part/analyse/render, part keys `<sha>_p<k>-<engine>`), PMI reading limited to the
chosen part, and an `assembly_drawn_whole` warning when an assembly is drawn without a part.
Single-part output is unchanged: every single-sheet SVG/PDF/PNG keeps its bytes; only the plan
hashes of five PMI fixture cases were re-approved. Review: one final round, three minors kept
as logged decisions (`--part 0` on a single-part file takes the STEP product name, not the
file stem; the new optional `part_index`/`parts` fields would be refused by older
deny_unknown_fields readers such as the editor's vendored copy; `pmi::Drawing::new` takes the

## j4-datum-baseline integration (2026-10-16)

Merged `j/j4-datum-baseline` (#33): on a part turned whole, a profile running end to end is
dimensioned as a baseline from one end instead of a chain when the PMI measures it from that end
(a planar PMI datum on that end face, or PMI lengths from it to the shoulders). Each further
shoulder becomes `d:f:turned_step:<n>/step_position.length` from the datum; the overall length is
the last rung, so nothing is drawn as reference, and a chain member that states PMI is never made
reference. Saved specs keep working: every band's `step.length` stays in the plan, covered by the
rung that locates its band and reserving no space (the editor's saved spindle spec otherwise
failed, and counting the space moved it from 1:1 A4 to 2:1 A3). On the review spindle the heights
go from a chain with a reference dimension to 12, 34 and 52 from A, still 1:1 A4;
`spindle_shaft_fit` goes from (12), 22, 18 to 12, 34 from A with 52 as the last rung.
Finding reported by the stream and confirmed by review: the spindle STEP has no PMI lengths for
12/34/42/52; the editor's CSV rows are ISO 2768-m general limits, and 42 is the tapped hole's
floor, already given by the callout depth. The literal acceptance case is covered with injected
PMI lengths. Final review: five minors, none blocking (the brief's premise; the non-reference
assertion also passes on base, though both new spindle tests fail on base; `spindle_shaft_fit`'s
change unmentioned; `datum_end` relies on the datum plane's normal sign, failing safe to the
chain; the ⌀15 g6 leader still runs along its outline, a J3 placement issue).
Integration: docs/next-session.md conflicted with J2 (both entries kept); no textual code
conflicts. CI's first run failed J2's `fix_relayout` test: it expected datum B's leader to cross
`d:f:turned_step:2/step.length` (22), which J4 replaces with the 34-from-A rung
`step_position.length`. The expected id was updated; every other assertion is unchanged and passes.
Local checks on the merged tree: fmt and strict all-target release Clippy pass; tests
pmi_dimensions 17, fix_relayout 2, `--test rendering sheets::` 28 (macOS, no re-recording needed); test_quality and
test_ci_acceptance pass; quality over 30 parts equal to tools/ci-quality-baseline.json in every
total; check_plan_time passes (0.755T of 3.401T instructions, Apple silicon). MCP and wire format
untouched.

## k2-speed-tail integration (2026-10-16)

Merged `k/k2-speed-tail`: the render-time tail of #43 item 3. The slow part was the render (the
plan takes 0.255T / 0.613T instructions), nearly all in haecceity's hidden-line projection of the
end-on top view, where thread turns and comb teeth stack up. The fix is in quiddity-rust
(`k/k2-hlr-speed`, 7cb26d8: narrower ray queries with full-list fallback, B-spline hit reuse,
nearer-boxes-first search, padded-box cell pre-test, one-pass basis functions, trim-test bins that
drop parallel segments). Every change gives the same answer; both tail SVGs are byte-identical.
draftwright pins quiddity/haecceity to 7cb26d8 and adds a render-tail acceptance job
(`tools/check_render_tail.py`, `tools/render-tail-budget.json`), a paused-child fix in
check_plan_time and a re-recorded plan budget (2.957T -> 2.632T). Figures (retired instructions,
Apple silicon): threaded_connector_109 render 23.502T -> 4.083T (5.76x), cgb243 12.716T ->
2.683T (4.74x, now 158 s wall). Final review: one major (merge order: the quiddity-rust commit was
unpushed), two minors (Linux processor-second ceilings are estimates and together exceed the job's
30-minute timeout; a theoretical rounding case in the padded-box pre-test, no output change).
Integration: pushed `k/k2-hlr-speed` and merged it into quiddity-rust main first (PR #12, merge
commit, so rev 7cb26d8 stays reachable; no re-pin needed); one conflict in docs/next-session.md

pass; `--test rendering sheets::` 28 (macOS, no fixture change); test_quality and test_ci_acceptance pass;

format untouched. Watch the first Linux render-tail run and tighten the ceilings.

fixture change); test_quality and test_ci_acceptance pass; MCP schemas regenerate with no diff;
scripted MCP evaluation 7/7; check_plan_time passes (2.632T of 3.027T, Apple silicon, budget not
re-recorded). quality.py not run locally (default output byte-identical; only opt-in Spec entries
draw anything new).

## k4-leader-rules integration (2026-10-16)

Merged `k/k4-leader-rules`: #43 item 2, leader-correctness rules from Python. The router's
material re-entry (a leader cutting back into the part) is now one shared predicate,
`src/material.rs`, used by the router (re-entry is one hard blocker, outranking crossings, with
its depth added to the length) and by new lint leader rules (`src/lint/leaders.rs`); lint's guard
allows `crate::material` and reads it too. The plan now reports a pinned callout's re-entry as
`annotation_line_over_view` with the unpin fix (`placement_intent::finish`, via
`dims::leader_reentry`), so plan and lint agree. New `tests/leader_rules.rs` (5 tests; 4 fail on
the base). Rules: 1 ADR, 5 lint and 4 issues rules ported. Review rounds: impl, one fix round (guard
test blocker, a misplaced doc comment, an agreement test that tested nothing and hid the pinned
re-entry bug, a note's wording). Final review: no blocker, three minors (the re-entry report is
skipped when the same callout already has an `annotation_line_over_view` problem for another
reason; `leader_reentry` rebuilds each view's footprints per pinned callout; ISSUE-367-a and
ARCH0017-6 stay pending, disclosed). Integration: merged onto main 253b7601 (after #49, the NTS
iso caption, and #53, the guard made opt-in); conflicts in docs/rules/README.md (recounted from
the merged YAML: 199 pending, 218 ported, 39 translated, 59 retired) and
tests/fixtures/known_single_sheet_approved.json (main's re-baselined, unmaintained fixture kept;
the stream's 5 plate-and-pin replacements dropped, since the guard is opt-in and its fixtures are
no longer re-recorded). Local checks: fmt and strict all-target release Clippy pass; lib 197,
leader_rules 5, issue_drafting 36, pinned_offset 5, pinned_callouts 3, placement_intent 13,
`--test rendering sheets::` 28; test_quality 40 and test_ci_acceptance 24 pass; quality.py over the 30
parts equals tools/ci-quality-baseline.json in every cell (ink 52, line_through_feature 73,
hidden_on_visible 2232, repeated_label 7, others 0, before and after); MCP schemas regenerate
with no diff; check_plan_time passes (2.667T of 3.027T, Apple silicon, budget not re-recorded).

## k5-completeness-rules integration (2026-10-16)

Merged `k/k5-completeness-rules`: #43 item 2, dimensioning-completeness rules from Python. Several
turned bodies on one axis now get one length chain each, from the body's own outline
(`dims::turned::own_body`, `placement::body_strip`); a groove naming none of several bodies is
refused (`feature_not_dimensioned`). New planners: raised pads (`src/dims/pads.rs`: footprint,
`N HIGH`, corner location both ways, via lint's pad reader) and free-direction slots
(`src/dims/oriented_slots.rs`: one `ORIENTED SLOT W WIDE × L LONG` callout, counted when equal).
Lint: any dimension spanning a whole axis states that extent (`lint/envelope.rs`); `N× v` credits
exactly N adjoining bands (`lint/turned.rs`). New `tests/completeness_rules.rs` (7 tests, all fail
on the base). Review rounds: impl, final review with no blocker and six minors (most acceptance
items honestly unmet and kept pending with blockers: pocket patterns, outer-profile angles,
ARCH0020-2, ISSUE-1357-c, ISSUE-1432-a, ISSUE-885-a, ISSUE-443-a; cosmetic crowding on the
turned-step-compound and pad-compound-equal fixtures; `grouped()` rejects lettered labels;
`grooves::owner` would drop a null-profile groove on a multi-body part). Integration: merged onto
main 3836b296 with no conflicts. Local checks: fmt and strict all-target release Clippy pass;
completeness_rules 7, dimensions 1, lint 10, lint_family_rest 1, lint_coverage 4, completeness
14, h5_chains 15, dims_turned 8, issue_drafting 36, leader_rules 5, lib lint::envelope 3,
`--test rendering sheets::` 28; test_quality 40 and test_ci_acceptance 24 pass; quality.py over the 30 parts
against tools/ci-quality-baseline.json: no cell worse, hidden_on_visible 2232 -> 2231
(string_post 26 -> 25), others unchanged; no MCP or wire-format change; check_plan_time passes
(2.708T of 3.027T, up from 2.667T, Apple silicon, budget not re-recorded).

## k6-one-owner integration (2026-10-16)

Merged `k/k6-one-owner`: one owner per measurement, no over-dimensioning (ARCH0015-2, ADR4-U1,
ISSUE-1505-a/b, ISSUE-883-a). An auto-mode omission of one ladder or shoulder-set member spreads
to the correlated set (`correlated_set_whole` info names the members and restores them), but never
over a member the spec decides (a tolerance, view, place or `omit: false`): that set is drawn as
stated with a `correlated_set_conflict` warning whose fix removes the omission. Redundancy lint
and planner changes in `src/dims/redundancy.rs`, `src/lint/redundancy.rs`,
`src/lint/hole_patterns.rs`. New `tests/ownership_rules.rs` (7 tests) and
`dims_turned::a_short_shoulder_never_erases_its_neighbours_lengths` (a regression pin that also
passes on the base). Review rounds: impl, one fix round (blocker: an omission overrode a member
with its own tolerance; fixed), final review with no blocker and four minors (ISSUE-1505-b pin
passes on the base; whole-ladder omission in auto mode kept per ARCH0015-2 and logged for the
maintainer, cgb245 loses ten rungs when one is omitted; boss_circle's toleranced 3 ±0.1 leaves a
closed chain, unchanged from base; `toleranced()` matches a fixed ISO 286 list). ISSUE-1505-a,
ISSUE-1505-b and ISSUE-883-a stay pending with decisions logged in docs/next-session.md.
Integration: merged onto main 53aa9f3f with no conflicts. CI's placement shard then failed
`omitting_one_dimension_moves_no_other` on ftc06, a test the stream had not run. The fix commit
stops an authored set's ladder approval from bringing back a rung that an item the set leaves out
conveys. It also extends the locality test so a correlated set counts as one item: siblings are
omitted with `correlated_set_whole`, and every other item is still checked as fixed. Both are
logged in docs/next-session.md. Local checks: fmt and strict
all-target release Clippy pass; ownership_rules 7, dims_turned 9, dims_steps 11, trust_rules 12,
issue_drafting 36, placement 9, completeness_rules 7, `--test rendering sheets::` 28; test_quality 40 and test_ci_acceptance 24 pass; quality.py
over the 30 parts against tools/ci-quality-baseline.json: no cell worse, identical to main
(hidden_on_visible 2231, ink 52, line_through_feature 73, repeated_label 7, others 0); MCP
schemas regenerate with no diff; check_plan_time passes (2.709T of 3.027T, unchanged from main,
Apple silicon, budget not re-recorded).

### K8 rules re-assessment (k/int-k8-rules-reassess, 2026-10-16)

Stream K8 re-assessed every pending rule in `docs/rules/` (on its branch: pending 188 to 160,
ported 229 to 237, translated 39 to 46, retired 59 to 72; 52 note-less pending rules given
notes; two renamed test citations repointed) and added `tests/rules_catalogue.rs` (6 tests:
every status backed by its field, every cited test existing, README tables equal to the files,
and scenarios for ADR2-U1, ARCH0009-5 and ISSUE-917-a). Review rounds: impl, final review with
no blocker and three minors (the README test passes on the base; four rules marked ported only
on the bridge path; the cited-test check matches names, not files). Integration: main had since
gained the maintainer-approved triage of all pending rules (#58), which conflicted in all three
YAML files and the README. Main is authoritative: every status, port_note, rust_test and the
README tables are main's (pending 80, ported 234, translated 130, retired 71), so K8's status
changes and its bridge-path "ported" calls do not land. K8 contributes the test file, its
next-session log (with an integration note) and `rust_note`s for the 15 PORT rules the triage
left with none, which the new test requires. No code changed. Local checks: fmt and strict
all-target release Clippy pass; rules_catalogue 6 and `--test rendering sheets::` 28 pass; test_quality 40
and test_ci_acceptance 24 pass. check_plan_time fails locally (3.408T of 3.027T, Apple silicon)
on a tree whose `src/` is identical to main 5b18a64d, against 2.709T at the K6 integration; the
regression is already on main (it came in after K6, with #56 or #58's base) and not this merge.
The budget was not re-recorded.

### Native section recesses: completeness reference refresh (cx/native-section-recesses, 2026-10-17)

Native recesses read quiddity's section recesses (recognition/3) and add them as
`section_recess` features. Their pocket-pattern members are drafted. The other
recess grammars (general pockets, rectangular channels and the rest) are not yet
dimensioned. They are reported `deferred` / `dimensions_not_ported`, so they count in
`unplanned_features`. Compared with main (adf92b4c) on the 30 quality parts:
`unplanned_features` rises on 21 parts, 56 features in total. Every one of the 56 is a
newly recognised `section_recess`. No feature main dimensioned loses a dimension.
`claimed_faces` rises on every one of those parts (e.g. 1000 10 → 49, CTC-01 77 → 107).
Against tools/ci-quality-baseline.json the drawings are 0 cells worse and 7 better
(ink 17 → 9, feature crossings 73 → 65). The table below is this build's
`tools/quality.py` run (target/status-refresh). It replaces the previous table as the
reference for `check_quality_table.py`. Every other column is no worse than the table it
replaces. Dimensioning the deferred section recesses (Python plans them, e.g. two
requirements on CTC-01) is the follow-up that brings `unplanned_features` back down.

### Identical slots share one width; slot location lint (2026-10-24)

Identical lone slots on different stretches share one counted width, and slot location lint
reads the low end the planner draws. Compared with the previous table: nist_ctc_03_asme1_rc
`repeated_label` 1 → 0 and `text_off_span` 1 → 0 (`2× 19.05`); nist_ctc_04_asme1_rd
`repeated_label` 2 → 0 and `line_through_feature` 22 → 19 (`2× 75`, `2× 140`), with
`text_off_span` 0 → 1: the `2× 75` label does not fit inside its 15 mm span at 1:5 and is
placed beside it, accepted for the two repeated labels and three extension crossings it
removes; nist_ftc_09_asme1_rd `repeated_label` 1 → 0 and `line_through_feature` 2 → 0
(`2× 6.35`). 1004 `line_through_feature` 2 → 1 is main's since the previous table. The

### quiddity-rust 0.2.0-alpha.2 (2026-10-25)

The move to quiddity-rust v0.2.0-alpha.2 (PR #202). Compared with the previous table, only
face claims change. 1000, 10033 and nist_ctc_03_asme1_rc each claim one face fewer
(`claimed_faces` 49 → 48, 17 → 16, 93 → 92): quiddity no longer publishes a concave round as
a convex fillet (pzfreo/quiddity-rust#89), so those faces are unclaimed rather than claimed
falsely; CTC-03 loses 6 false fillets and gains 5 blends. cgb207 claims one more face
(32 → 33) and has no unplanned feature (1 → 0), as its R5 fillet is read on a file axis again.
The leader routes of 10033, cgb207 and cgb241 are re-recorded in
`tests/fixtures/leader_routes.json`. cgb241's `2× R23` is now led from its fillet: 71 mm with
three hidden-line crossings, against 37 mm and none from the blend on main, accepted. The table
below is CI's quality-gate measurement of this branch and replaces the previous table as the
reference for `check_quality_table.py`.

### Claims follow quiddity's face accounting (PR #209, 2026-10-25)

For #207, a face is claimed when quiddity's `face_accounting` gives it to any record, evidence
records such as `step_levels` included, and its `body` faces are claimed once the overall sizes
are stated. The envelope-plane stock rule now applies only to analyses without accounting. Only
`claimed_faces` changes. Parts whose envelope planes quiddity calls `unexplained` claim fewer
faces: 1000 48 → 47, 10007 18 → 16, 1004 27 → 24, 10046 24 → 23, 10047 34 → 33,
10049 30 → 28, 10060 26 → 24, 10063 35 → 34, GRM-03 12 → 11, GRM-05 7 → 6, CTC-01 115 → 114,
FTC-06 101 → 96 and string_post 16 → 14 (string_post now claims exactly its 14 `feature` faces;
its other 14 are `unexplained`). These faces return when pzfreo/quiddity-rust#100 adds body
evidence. Parts with faces quiddity accounts to records draftwright imports only as evidence,
or not at all, claim more: for example cgb203 3 → 154, cgb207 33 → 202, cgb245 176 → 450 and
sm-hanger 47 → 91. The CTC-04 ink, line-through and leader-on-extension improvements come
from PR #205, already on main. The table below is CI's quality-gate measurement of this
branch and replaces the previous table as the reference for `check_quality_table.py`.

### quiddity-rust v0.2.0 and inch PMI (PR #229, 2026-10-26)

For #207 and #214. quiddity-rust v0.2.0's face accounting (blended box corners, plate edges,
stock walls, drill-point tips, slot end walls) raises `claimed_faces` on 17 of the 30 parts,
recovering the losses recorded for PR #209 and more: for example 10007 16 → 20, 10049 28 → 33,
CTC-01 114 → 136, CTC-02 325 → 473, CTC-04 246 → 290 and FTC-06 96 → 101. Newly recognised
steps change three layouts. 10033 moves from A4 to A3 2:1 and gains one leader crossing a
dimension line (ink 0 → 1). FTC-10 rb gains one extension line through a feature (10 → 11).
10060's section-recess pocket width leader is 2 mm longer. CTC-04 loses one line through a
feature and cgb241 one leader on an extension line. On the FTC-10 AP242 drawing, leader-to-text
and leader-to-dimension-line crossings each rise by one while the total falls from 159 to 149.
The quiddity-only build gives the same counts, so the PMI change (#214) moves none of them. The
leader crossings, the 10060 leader route and the table below are CI's quality-gate measurement
of this branch, and the table replaces the previous one as the reference for
`check_quality_table.py`.

### Backlog stack: #215, #218, #221, #227, #228 and zero deviations (2026-10-26)

For #215, #218, #221, #227 and #228, with Spec tolerances' zero deviation printed as `0`. The
30-part table is unchanged: every cell of CI's quality-gate measurement of this stack equals the
previous table, and the nine random-pin cells are GO (string_post's known #206 NO-GO aside). The
one ratchet change is on the NIST FTC-07 AP242 drawing, from #215: with the surface-model fills
left out of the envelope its front-view box is 17.5 mm shorter, the A2 1:2 layout is refused and
the drawing moves to A1 1:1. There leader-to-extension-line crossings rise by one (13 → 14) while
leader-to-dimension-line crossings fall from 19 to 15 and leader-to-arrow crossings from 10 to
8, so its total falls from 52 to 47. `tools/leader-crossings-baseline.json` is re-recorded from
this measurement. #217 (cut corners located by their legs) was left out of the stack: on its own
it raises FTC-08 rc's `repeated_label` from 1 to 5 (the eight octagon stations repeat in
symmetric pairs) and its seed 2, 8 pin cell goes from 0 to 30 ink items (NO-GO).

### Annotation contacts, bolt circles, hole marks and counted callouts (2026-10-26)

For #219, #220, #223, #224, #225 and #226, measured on their stacked branch by CI's quality-pins
and quality-gate jobs, run locally. One cell changes. CTC-04's `ink` rises from 0 to 1: #226's
new contact check reports `label_section_furniture_overlap` for the plan view's "2× 285.28" slot
length, whose dimension line runs through the section A cutting-plane arrowhead. #226 moves no
annotation, and that dimension's label box is the same on #226 alone and on the stack, so the
count reports a contact that was already drawn, not a new one. No other cell moves: #219's
assembly rule leaves the single-body quality parts alone, and #220's groove and boss changes, #223's
bolt circles and grid centre marks, #224's attached frames and datums, and #225's wording all
leave every count the same. Leader crossings, the leader routes and every random-pin cell pass
unchanged. The table below replaces the previous one as the reference for
`check_quality_table.py`.

With batch 1 (#234) beneath it and the quiddity-rust v0.2.1-alpha.1 pin, leader crossings are
re-recorded from CI's measurement of PR #235. They fall on CTC-01 AP242 (leader to dimension line
5 → 2, C's remote front-view leader gone now that #224 attaches C to its hole callout), CTC-02
AP242 (leader to arrow 10 → 8, to dimension line 12 → 11, leader to leader 3 → 1) and FTC-10

15 → 19 on CI only: its all-over profile-surface frame lands at a different spot in the plan on
x86-64 Linux than on Apple Silicon (15 there), a placement near-tie the platforms break
differently. The layout is otherwise identical; the cross-platform tie is a finding to follow up.

### Unexplained shapes, recesses, squaring, idle views and hole fixes (2026-10-26)

For #213, #216, #222, #223, #224, #226, #227, #233 and #236, measured on their stacked branch by
CI's quality-pins and quality-gate jobs, run locally. Three cells change, and only one comes from
this stack. 10060's `ink` falls from 1 to 0: #222 leaves out its empty plan view, so the drawing
goes from A4 1:1 to A4 2:1 and its one annotation leader crossing goes. CTC-01's `claimed_faces`
rises from 136 to 139 and FTC-07's from 109 to 113, but those counts are main's own: the #233,
#216 and #236 branches, each main plus its own change, measure 139 and 113 alone, so the previous
table predates them. No other cell moves. #213 changes findings only (`shape_not_recognised`,
a warning with impact `incomplete`), and #216's squaring, #223's hole fixes, #224's attachments,
#226's contact checks, #227's angle placement and #236's 75 mm tie leave every count the same.

#222 also takes 10020 and 10046 from A4 1:1 to A4 2:1. At 2:1 their leaders are longer on paper,
and leader routes are re-recorded for those parts and 10060: 10020's boss ⌀ leader 9.0 → 15.4 mm,
its chamfer leader 11.5 → 21.0 mm with 2 hidden-line crossings (0 before), and pocket widths
10.5 → 16.3 mm and 9.4 → 12.0 mm (1 hidden crossing); 10046's pocket width 27.8 → 46.9 mm; 10060's
ramp angles 28.5 → 51.0 mm and 13.2 → 20.9 mm, one with 4 hidden crossings (1 before), and its
pocket width 16.0 → 22.2 mm. These are synthetic parts and the larger scale is the gain.
Random-pin cell seed 3, 8 pins is listed as a known NO-GO for 10060 in
`tools/known-pin-failures.json`. Its pins name the side and iso views, and naming a principal view
keeps every view, so the pinned drawing keeps main's A4 1:1 and its one crossing. Against the
improved unpinned drawing, which has none, that fails the pin bar. Leader crossings and every other
pin cell pass unchanged. The CI quality baseline is recorded again from this run. Besides 10060's
ink, its sheet choices pick up the three 2:1 scales and three entries that were stale on main:
GRM-03 at A4 5:1 on one sheet, FTC-08 rc on one A1 sheet and FTC-10 rb on two A2 sheets. A branch
binary from main measures the same for each. #217 (cut corners located by their legs) is left out
of this stack: it adds an `extension_through_feature` to FTC-08 rc (`line_through_feature` 0 → 1),
and it takes STC-08 from A1 1:1 to A2 1:5. The table below replaces the previous one as the
reference for `check_quality_table.py`.

### Drawing review fixes (2026-10-27)

Six branches from the dw-examples drawing review: false bolt circles (#223), hole sizes that also
name the hole's rim edge, cited datums on a width or read through a nonconformance, the refused
modelled thread on the T8 flange nut, PMI text fixes (degree signs, lone-symbol labels, the
assembly note), and sheet choice judged by drawn ink (#163). No cell of the quality table moves:
the 30 parts measure exactly as in the previous table, which the table below repeats as the
reference for `check_quality_table.py`. The only change in the CI quality baseline is sm-hanger's
paper, A3 1:2 to A4 1:2 (#163: its unused principal band past the border no longer refuses the
smaller sheet). Leader routes, leader crossings, label-lane locality and all nine random-pin
cells pass against main's records, so nothing else is recorded.

On the 35 dw-examples drawings, 15 change. gantry-plate goes from A3 2:1 to A4 1:1 with
`7× ⌀5.1 THRU` in place of the false bolt circle; corner-bracket-2020 from A4 1:1 to A4 2:1;
t8-flange-nut's bore reads `⌀8.3 × P2 4 STARTS`; gt2-pulley and lm8uu carry the assembly note
(the pulley's isometric is then at sheet scale and loses its NTS caption); CTC-01's angle prints
`60° ±0.5°`. On the NIST files cited datums are drawn (frame_datum_not_drawn: STC-06 13 to 1,
STC-08 9 to 2, FTC-06 4 to 0, CTC-02 3 to 2) and more PMI is lowered (STC-10 35 to 29, FTC-06 52
to 47, STC-07 57 to 54). Their costs are cosmetic leader crossings on dense NIST sheets (FTC-06 3
to 14, STC-07 4 to 12) and CTC-03's hole table: with datum D drawn the dw-examples CTC-03 locates
its holes individually on A3 1:5, completing its hole-pattern requirements (4 to 0) at the price
of a busier plan view.

### Drawing review fixes, round 2 (2026-10-27)

Three branches from the second dw-examples drawing review: cut profile corners located by their
legs with linear stations instead of an unlocated angle (#217, cx/e7), congruent obround slots
dimensioned as slots (cx/e8), and turned-part callouts: coaxial bores located by the axis,
`BOTH SIDES` on counterbores, leaders kept off shared centre lines (cx/e9).

Changed cells against the previous table:

- 1004 `line_through_feature` 1 to 0 and CTC-02 4 to 2 (#217): the linear corner stations replace
  dimensions whose lines ran through features.
- cgb245 `line_through_feature` 3 to 1, `repeated_label` 1 to 0 and `leader_on_extension` 2 to 0
  (slots): its slots are called out once as slots instead of pieced together from widths,
  lengths and end radii.
- CTC-03 `line_through_feature` 1 to 0 (slots): same cause.
- FTC-09 `ink` 0 to 1 and `leader_on_extension` 0 to 1 (slots): its slot leader no longer runs
  through a slot, but now crosses slot 3's `38.1` extension line. That dimension's extension and
  dimension lines box the two slots below slot 3 in, so every route out crosses something. This
  one cosmetic crossing is accepted for the slot callouts, as the changelog records.

Totals: `repeated_label` 2 to 1, `ink` 2 to 3, `line_through_feature` 49 to 43,
`leader_on_extension` 5 to 4. No part gains an off-sheet item, a view overlap or a lost face.
In the CI quality baseline, two paper choices also change. Corpus FTC-08 goes from A1 1:2 to
A2 1:2: #217's reserved station lanes keep the plan view's left strip from overflowing. MFCAD
10046 goes from A4 2:1 to A4 1:1: it gains a plan view and linear corner stations (`13.978`,
`18.759`) in place of three angles. 10046 is a synthetic part, so the smaller scale is accepted
for the located corners. Leader crossings are recorded again: FTC-09 gains its one
`leader_extension_line`, and 10060, CTC-02 (AP242) and FTC-07 (AP242) lose crossings. The leader
route record changes for FTC-09 only.

The random-pin harness now pins every candidate when a part has fewer drawable candidates than
the cell asks for. Removing cgb203's repeated `180` leaves it 7 candidates, and the 8-pin cells
used to stop on an error. The validator now checks the selection against the recorded candidate
count.

Of the 35 dw-examples drawings, 19 change. Nothing worsens beyond cosmetic leader costs:

- nema17-bracket moves from A3 2:1 to A4 1:1, with `6× SLOT 4.5 × 7.5`.
- vslot-2020x50 moves from A4 1:1 to A3 2:1, with its profile located by `2× 1.8`, `2× 5.15` and
  `2× 6.95` instead of 23 unlocated angles.
- l-plate-5-holes, nema17-mount-bracket and NIST STC-08 have their chamfered corners located by
  linear stations.
- gantry-plate and STC-09 use slot callouts.
- dw-examples CTC-03 gets its hole table back, on a much cleaner plan view.
- FTC-08's callout pile to the right of the plan view is gone.
- The bearings, gt2-pulley, hirth-joint, the sprocket, FTC-11 and STC-06 keep their bore leaders
  off centre lines.
  hirth-joint also drops two redundant `10` dimensions that located a coaxial bore.
- lm10uu, FTC-10 and STC-10 now say `BOTH SIDES` on their counterbores.

### Drawing review fixes, round 3 (2026-10-28)

This stack merges five drawing-review branches: 45° profile corner chamfers called out by size
(FTC-08 station on its own line, STC-08 back at 1:1), CTC-01's single `225` with bosses located
on a hole's location line, PMI hole callouts replaced by table rows with `↧ 10` kept together,
split full-round slots dimensioned by their end centres, and half sections for turned parts with
stepped bores. One merge fix was needed. The boss-location audit imported the planner's
`SAME_SPAN`, which the lint guard forbids, so the constant now lives in lint and dims re-uses it.
Hole table rows also no longer convey a boss location. A row has no witness line, and counting
the rows made STC-07's four bosses read as located twice.

All 300 quality cells match the round 2 table exactly. The leader crossings and leader routes
pass against their records unchanged, so nothing is recorded again. One paper choice changes:
the FTC-08 quality part goes from A2 1:2 to A1 1:2 and gains `detail_unplaceable` (its
dimensions stay on the parent view). Its plan view is cleaner with `4× C21.075`, but the sheet is
mostly empty. This is cosmetic by the review bar and is left open.

Of the 35 dw-examples drawings, 10 change and none gets worse beyond cosmetic costs:

- FTC-08 gets its hole table, and its plan-view dimension pile goes. On the same A2 1:5 sheet,
  `extension_through_feature` goes 32 to 13 and `annotation_leader_crossing` drops by 15.
- CTC-01 draws `225` once, and its hex boss counts as located.
- STC-07 and STC-10 replace their PMI hole callouts with the table rows.
- FTC-10 and STC-10 break their counterbore callouts before `↧ 10`.
- STC-06's bosses 2 and 4 count as located.
- STC-08's four `nominal_rounded` notes go.
- l-plate-5-holes gets `C40`.

### quiddity-rust v0.2.2 (2026-10-28)

draftwright now pins quiddity-rust v0.2.2. Two recognition changes reach the drawings. A near-side
hole step deeper than 1 mm is a counterbore, not a spotface (quiddity-rust#118), and its new
`outer_steps` are called out as further `⌴` segments. Straight convex rounds between tangent walls
are fillets at any wall angle (quiddity-rust#120). Oblique fillets are now called out like oriented
chamfers: counted on the callout of equal principal fillets, or called out where their edge is seen
most nearly end-on. Without that, an undrawn exception withdrew the `ALL UNSPECIFIED RADII` note.

Seven cells change; one is worse:

- cgb207 claimed_faces 202 → 211: rounds that were unexplained are now fillets and are called out.
- cgb241 leader_on_extension 1 → 0: its R173 and 2× R23 rounds become fillet callouts, R170
  moves into the radius table, and the callouts placed afresh leave no leader on an extension
  line.
- CTC-02 line_through_feature 2 → 0 and claimed_faces 473 → 503: blends become fillets and the
  `⌴SF ⌀120 ↧ 8` callouts become `⌴ ⌀120 ↧ 8`. Their leaders get 6 mm longer and cross two more
  hidden lines; the leader routes are recorded again for that and for cgb241's.
- CTC-03 claimed_faces 102 → 103, CTC-04 290 → 296 and FTC-08 138 → 150: more fillets.
- CTC-05 claimed_faces 167 → 163 (worse): quiddity no longer reads the four R203.2 cylinders as
  fillets, because their walls are grossly non-tangent. The `4× R203.2` callout goes and the four
  faces are unclaimed. This is quiddity's decision (its v0.2.2 notes name the faces), so the
  cell is recorded as it is.

The CTC-02 AP242 drawing's datum F leader moves to the right of the front view. Its
leader-to-dimension-line crossings go 3 → 10 and its leader-to-arrow crossings 2 → 7. These
are cosmetic on a dense NIST part, so they are recorded in the leader-crossings baseline. CTC-04
leader-to-leader crossings go 10 → 8. The CI quality baseline is recorded again too: CTC-02's
line_through_feature is 0, and FTC-08's paper is A1 1:2, as it already was on main.

### FTC-10: frames attach to their holes, duplicate callouts go (2026-10-29)

A frame or datum symbol no longer keeps a PMI hole callout beside its hole table row: a frame
with no clear place on its holes' callout or tag takes a leader to one of the holes (with their
count over it when it controls several), and a datum symbol stands on such a frame of its hole.
The 30 quality drawings are unchanged (none has a frame on a hole). Leader crossings are
re-recorded for FTC-10 AP242 only: leader to leader 42 → 46, to extension line 46 → 50, to
arrowhead 26 → 29, to dimension line 29 → 28, through text 5 → 4. Its three front-view PMI
callouts are gone and the freed layout moves the lowered symmetry and plan-view position frames
(their crossings 4 → 11 and 6 → 12); the ⌀7 holes' `4× ⌀0.0Ⓜ` frame, now on a leader to its
hole, loses its 6. The frames moved by this change cross nothing; a leader to a hole is refused
where it would cross another item. Within the dense-NIST cosmetic bar.

### Drawing review fixes, round 4 (2026-10-29)

This stack merges four drawing-review branches: multi-step counterbore callouts read one step
per line (g1), a lone isometric returns to sheet 1 and keeps a 6 mm gutter from tables (g2),
frames on holes attach to their holes and duplicate PMI callouts go (g3), and the automatic half
section covers more turned parts (g4). The only conflict was CHANGELOG.md, where both sides were
kept. No merge-fix commit was needed.

All 300 quality cells match the v0.2.2 table exactly, so no cell changes. The CI quality baseline
(metrics and paper choices) already matches the fresh measurement; g2 recorded the FTC-08 (rc)
move from A1 to A2 1:2. The leader crossings and leader routes pass against their records
unchanged (g3 had already re-recorded the FTC-10 AP242 crossings), so nothing is recorded again.

Of the 35 dw-examples drawings, 8 change and none gets worse beyond cosmetic costs:

- CTC-04 moves from A0 to A1 at 1:5, still one sheet (g2). `leader_through_feature` 24 to 22.
- CTC-03, FTC-11: the isometric moves clear of the table, as the 6 mm gutter requires (g2).
- CTC-05 drops `⌀254 ±0.0254 ↧ 92.08` beside row B1; its runout frame and datum B lead to the
  hole (g3). `label_crowded` 3 to 2, `leader_to_hidden_edge` 1 to 0.
- FTC-07: a front-view position frame moves under the view on a short `2×` leader to its holes
  (g3). Leader crossings drop by 5.
- FTC-10 loses its three front-view PMI callouts (g3). Readability findings 122 to 120,
  `label_crowded` 6 to 3, `annotation_ink_overlap` 11 to 8; leader-to-leader and leader-to-
  extension crossings rise by 3 and 4, within the dense-NIST cosmetic bar.
- STC-10's frames lead to their holes (g3). `leader_through_feature` 53 to 38, readability
  findings 127 to 105.
- lm8uu draws its side view half cut, the wall under each ⌀13.4 groove a section edge (g4).

### quiddity-rust v0.2.3 (2026-10-29)

draftwright now pins quiddity-rust v0.2.3 (recognition/6 and fingerprints/3). Complete
`turned_bodies` establish the turning axis and overall diameter even when recognition no longer
publishes the nearly full-length OD as a boss. Each diameter is still stated once; the length,
shoulders, bores and grooves keep their dimensions. Body face evidence is credited only after
the overall extents are stated, rather than treating the body's co-owned faces as independently
dimensioned features. `face_grooves` use the annular recess grammar, including both rim
diameters, depth and each sloping wall angle. `cylindrical_profiles` use the existing counted
radius grammar and its Spec overrides; concave fillets share equal-radius principal fillet
callouts, as oblique fillets already do. The native family catalogue and strict reader include
all three families; the generated MCP schemas are unchanged.

The 608ZZ now states its OD once, its bore and length, and `2× ⌀18.5 × ⌀13 ↧ 0.25` with
OUTER/INNER 45.000° walls, on A4. The four nominal-rounding notices retain the provider's actual
44.999972° angles rather than silently replacing the measurements by exact 45°. Its shield
recesses can be read end-on, so they do not alone satisfy the existing automatic half-section
rule (a section must show what hidden lines cannot show cleanly). `views/front/half_section:
true` does cut it. An explicit Spec origin retains box-based locations. LM10UU and LM8UU keep
their half sections and every OD, bore, groove, shoulder and length size. CTC-05 again states
`4× R203.2`, alongside the newly recognised `2× R19.05` and `R127` profiles.

Pin replay retains the finished drawing's radius routes and hole schedule through forced-paper
trials. A view pin naming an already displayed view keeps that published composition. This
fixes the two FTC-08 eight-pin cases where an intermediate radius route prevented the schedule
from installing and reinstated crossing bore leaders. Both cases now have zero ink conflicts;
the radius/view-pin regression checks the complete schedule, source measurements and held
geometry. Explicit placement and hole-table settings remain authoritative.
The replay reference retains the reservations from before datum attachment grows them, so a
bare datum pin does not reserve its strip twice or move the attached glyph.

All 300 enforced quality cells (thirty parts × ten metrics) are unchanged from main 5599161c.
The fresh table below retains the fifteen integer columns checked by the status-table gate.
The current tool also reports interior-label counts and footprint fill in the external audit.
Every changed cell among the preceding table's columns is accounted for here:

- `claimed_faces`: 1000 50 → 51 and 10033 22 → 23 are newly stated cylindrical cuts. GRM-03
  11 → 14 and GRM-05 6 → 9 gain complete turned-body evidence, with OD and length already
  drawn. cgb207 211 → 234 gains concave rounds and cylindrical profiles.
- `claimed_faces`: CTC-02 503 → 539, CTC-04 296 → 381 and CTC-05 163 → 180; FTC-06 101 → 117,
  FTC-07 113 → 166, FTC-08 150 → 220 and FTC-10 114 → 155; sm-hanger 91 → 95. These are the
  new radius records and reclassified concave fillets, all retained in counted callouts.
- `leader_on_extension`: cgb241 0 → 1 and FTC-10 0 → 1. cgb241's now-counted `3× R23` route
  retains a 3.345 mm run within the 0.3 mm neighbourhood of model/extension ink. FTC-10's
  `⌀5.5 ↧ 27.5` leader grows 20.513 → 24.271 mm and retains a 3.199 mm run. These are
  recorded cosmetic residuals, with every size retained and no increase in ink overlaps.

Two paper choices change: FTC-08 is one A3 sheet at 1:5 instead of A2 at 1:2; FTC-10 is one
A1 sheet at 1:1 instead of two A2 sheets. The former keeps the smaller clean sheet; the latter
keeps the complete dimension inventory and section together. The slot-end radius shafts remain
exactly radial. A shallow FTC-08 radius-label/schedule-tag overlap was fixed with a small radial
extension; final ink remains zero. Final radius polishing now respects centre strokes, including
a stroke's end when the shaft overlaps it; cgb207's independent centre-stroke test passes.

The leader-route recorder updates six reviewed parts. Besides cgb241 and FTC-10 above: 1000's
HEX leader grows 9 → 12.930 mm around its new R7.179 callout; CTC-01's bore leader grows
16.252 → 18.473 mm beside the explicit `18× R10`; CTC-02's bore/seat leaders grow
49.625 → 52.235 and 66.403 → 69.002 mm, the seat crossing one additional hidden edge and
one cutting-plane stroke; FTC-08's slot-radius leaders grow 10 → 24 and 10 → 15.933 mm on
the smaller sheet, the latter crossing two hidden edges. The new radius sizes are not removed
to recover the previous routes. 1000 retains a reported non-radial radius fallback and the
structural review's legitimate interior-label and existing 0.0005 mm rounding notices.

The thirty quality parts' six crossing counts are unchanged. In the extra AP242 ratchet,
CTC-01 leader/leader crossings fall 2 → 0; FTC-10 leader/leader 26 → 22 and leader/arrow
19 → 13. FTC-07's new `47× R1.524` and `16× R3.048` callouts rearrange the datum B/D and
control-frame strips on A2 1:2: leader/leader 4 → 6, leader/dimension-line 4 → 8 and
leader/arrow 2 → 3. This retains all PMI and the new radii on the smaller sheet; no leader/text
crossing is introduced. The crossing baseline records these explicit differences.

Across thirty corpus drawings and thirty-five dw-examples, thirty-six drawings change ink or
view data, and the two GRM drawings additionally lose an unclaimed-geometry notice with
unchanged ink. PDFs and full render problem-code comparisons remain outside the repository.
Remaining dense NIST layout findings are retained and reported, including the FTC-08 AP242
bore-label overlaps and the extra radius/PMI crossings. More `shape_not_recognised` groups on
CTC-02 and FTC-10 are fragmentation of smaller residual face sets: CTC-02's unexplained set
shrinks 163 → 127 and FTC-10 AP242's 122 → 85, with no newly unexplained faces in either.

The native semantic difference ledger is refreshed without relabelling existing wrong or
undetermined cases. The PMI ledger removes the bolt's stale runout-view difference and records
STC-09 datum C on faces 24/25 and FTC-10 datum E on faces 242/243 at their hole control frames
in the front view. These are retained source requirements, not claims of Python drawing parity.

### quiddity-rust v0.2.5 (2026-10-30)

draftwright now pins quiddity-rust v0.2.5 (recognition/6 and fingerprints/3). For
quiddity-rust#134, boss height and free-end location include the boss's convex end treatments.
The planner uses those complete intervals directly: no chamfer or round is added a second
time. Edge-treated supporting plates remain plates; their thickness is stated by the existing
size or chain. Corrected concave turned fillets retain their established radius presenter,
exact counts and physical routes, alongside equal principal fillets. Correcting the side alone
must not move a valid leader or force larger paper; CTC-04 retains two A2 sheets at 1:5.

All thirty quality parts and all forty-four supplied dw-examples Specs were compared with
immutable main ecc65f43 (quiddity v0.2.3). Three drawings change ink, all retaining their paper:

- `hex-standoff-m3`: boss height 5.5 becomes the full 6; the coincident level's 6 is conveyed by
  that same dimension. `C0.5` remains independent and the overall length stays 18.
- `linkrods`: each boss now spans the full 2, so the overall 2 conveys both instead of printing
  a separate `2× 1.75` to the round starts. `4× R0.125` and all diameters and locations remain.
- `t2c-circular-flange`: the overall 10 conveys the complete boss height instead of adding a
  separate 7 between the round starts. `2× R1.5`, both diameters and the bolt circle remain.

Every render problem-code count is unchanged in those three drawings. Their existing section
furniture/crowding, extension-through-feature and centre-line leader notices remain explicit;
no new layout warning is introduced. Twenty-five other Plan pairs change only the provider
version reference or GRM-05's new plate-thickness omission; their drawing ink is unchanged.
The 14 mm GRM-05 head is already stated by its turned-step length, so no extra size is printed.
PDF pairs and the complete per-code comparisons remain in the external audit directory.

The native comparison pairs bosses by their physical cylinder faces before comparing every
parameter, because the corrected free end can otherwise match the next coaxial boss.
The refreshed semantic ledger records the full connector109 height, projected plate centres,
GRM-05's retained plate and actual turned-fillet sides. Existing wrong/undetermined verdicts
are unchanged. Schemas regenerate without changes; fingerprint values are regenerated by
native recognition within /3, without replacing historical Python or schema-migration captures.

All 450 integer cells below are unchanged from the preceding table. The fresh baseline's
CTC-02 paper record changes A1 to A2 at 1:5; immutable current main already uses A2, so this
corrects stale baseline metadata rather than changing paper in this pin. The 178-callout route
